     1	import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
     2	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     3	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
     4	import { cmdDetect } from './commands/detect.mjs';
     5	import { cmdRemap } from './commands/remap.mjs';
     6	import { cmdCheckpoint } from './commands/checkpoint.mjs';
     7	import { cmdFinalize } from './commands/finalize.mjs';
     8	import { cmdReceive } from './commands/receive.mjs';
     9	import { cmdInit } from './commands/init.mjs';
    10	import { cmdDoctor } from './commands/doctor.mjs';
    11	import { cmdPurgeTranscript } from './commands/purge-transcript.mjs';
    12	import { cmdSessionStart } from './commands/session-start.mjs';
    13	import { cmdRecover } from './commands/recover.mjs';
    14	import { cmdStatus } from './commands/status.mjs';
    15	
    16	// The ten user-facing commands are the frozen v1 contract; session-start is an
    17	// adapter-facing addition prescribed by gate-2 finding 8 (the cheap
    18	// SessionStart gate Codex/Cursor hooks call — always exit 0).
    19	const COMMANDS = [
    20	  'checkpoint',
    21	  'finalize',
    22	  'detect',
    23	  'remap',
    24	  'receive',
    25	  'init',
    26	  'doctor',
    27	  'status',
    28	  'purge-transcript',
    29	  'recover',
    30	  'session-start',
    31	];
    32	
    33	function version() {
    34	  const pkgPath = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'package.json');
    35	  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(pkgPath, 'utf8')).version;
    36	}
    37	
    38	const USAGE = `usage: baton <command> [options]
    39	
    40	commands:
    41	  ${COMMANDS.join(', ')}
    42	
    43	global options:
    44	  --version   print version
    45	  --json      machine-readable output: one compact JSON envelope on stdout
    46	`;
    47	
    48	/**
    49	 * The --json contract: exactly one compact envelope line on stdout, nothing else.
    50	 * @param {any} io
    51	 * @param {{ok: boolean, data?: any, warnings?: any[], error?: {code: string, msg: string} | null}} env
    52	 */
    53	function emitEnvelope(io, env) {
    54	  const full = { ok: env.ok, data: env.data ?? null, warnings: env.warnings ?? [], error: env.error ?? null };
    55	  io.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(full) + '\n');
    56	}
    57	
    58	/**
    59	 * @param {string[]} argv process args after the binary
    60	 * @param {any} io injected {cwd, stdout, stderr}
    61	 * @returns {number | Promise<number>} exit code
    62	 */
    63	export function run(argv, io) {
    64	  const args = argv.filter((a) => a !== '');
    65	  // Global --json intent is parsed BEFORE dispatch (gate-2 iter-2 M6): a
    66	  // top-level `--json` with no command, or --help/--version, must still emit
    67	  // exactly one envelope rather than raw text.
    68	  const jsonWanted = args.includes('--json');
    69	  const cmdIndex = args.findIndex((a) => !a.startsWith('--'));
    70	  const cmd = cmdIndex === -1 ? undefined : args[cmdIndex];
    71	
    72	  if (cmd === undefined) {
    73	    // No command — just flags (or nothing).
    74	    if (args.includes('--version')) {
    75	      if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { version: version() } });
    76	      else io.stdout.write(version() + '\n');
    77	      return 0;
    78	    }
    79	    if (args.includes('--help')) {
    80	      if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { usage: USAGE.trim() } });
    81	      else io.stdout.write(USAGE);
    82	      return 0;
    83	    }
    84	    // Bare invocation (or a lone --json): a usage error.
    85	    if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: 'no command given' } });
    86	    else io.stdout.write(USAGE);
    87	    return 2;
    88	  }
    89	
    90	  // All flags (before and after the command token) reach the command; only the
    91	  // command token itself is stripped, so a leading `--json` isn't lost.
    92	  const rest = args.filter((_, i) => i !== cmdIndex);
    93	
    94	  // Usage errors honor the --json envelope contract too (gate-2 fix 11 + M6:
    95	  // global --json counts, wherever it sits).
    96	  const wantsJson = jsonWanted;
    97	  if (cmd === 'wrap') {
    98	    if (wantsJson) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: 'baton wrap is reserved for a future release (PTY supervisor); not available in v1' } });
    99	    else io.stderr.write(`baton wrap is reserved for a future release (PTY supervisor); not available in v1.\n${USAGE}`);
   100	    return 2;
   101	  }
   102	  if (!COMMANDS.includes(cmd)) {
   103	    if (wantsJson) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: `unknown command '${cmd}'` } });
   104	    else io.stderr.write(`baton: unknown command '${cmd}'\n${USAGE}`);
   105	    return 2;
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  if (rest.includes('--help')) {
   109	    // Subcommand help honors the global --json envelope too (iter-3 F8): with
   110	    // --json it must emit exactly one envelope, never raw usage text.
   111	    const usage = `usage: baton ${cmd} [options]\n\nSee README for the ${cmd} contract.`;
   112	    if (wantsJson) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { usage } });
   113	    else io.stdout.write(`${usage}\n`);
   114	    return 0;
   115	  }
   116	
   117	  if (cmd === 'status') return cmdStatus(rest, io);
   118	  if (cmd === 'detect') return cmdDetect(rest, io);
   119	  if (cmd === 'remap') return cmdRemap(rest, io);
   120	  if (cmd === 'checkpoint') return cmdCheckpoint(rest, io);
   121	  if (cmd === 'finalize') return cmdFinalize(rest, io);
   122	  if (cmd === 'receive') return cmdReceive(rest, io);
   123	  if (cmd === 'init') return cmdInit(rest, io);
   124	  if (cmd === 'doctor') return cmdDoctor(rest, io);
   125	  if (cmd === 'purge-transcript') return cmdPurgeTranscript(rest, io);
   126	  if (cmd === 'session-start') return cmdSessionStart(rest, io);
   127	  if (cmd === 'recover') return cmdRecover(rest, io);
   128	
   129	  const error = { code: 'not-implemented', msg: `baton ${cmd}: not implemented yet` };
   130	  if (rest.includes('--json')) {
   131	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error });
   132	  } else {
   133	    io.stderr.write(`${error.msg}\n`);
   134	  }
   135	  return 1;
   136	}

    12	const BUILTIN_SIGNATURES = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
    13	const STALE_AFTER_MS = 12 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

    15	/** @typedef {{platform: string, origin: string, reason: string, gitSnapshot: any, probes?: any, sessionHint: string, sessionUnstable?: boolean, reasonClass?: string}} ReceiveOpts */
    16	
    17	/**
    18	 * Classify the switch reason, KEEPING confidence (gate-2 iter-2 M4): a
    19	 * low-confidence heuristic (e.g. Cursor's "quota exceeded") must not silently
    20	 * drive an avoid[] decision — the caller warns and defers to explicit intake.
    21	 * @param {string} reason @param {string} originPlatform @param {any} io
    22	 * @returns {{class: string | null, confidence: string | null}}
    23	 */
    24	function classifyReason(reason, originPlatform, io) {
    25	  try {
    26	    const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
    27	    const verdict = classify({ text: reason, exitCode: 0, platform: originPlatform, table, structured: null });
    28	    return verdict.class === 'ok' ? { class: null, confidence: null } : { class: verdict.class, confidence: verdict.confidence };
    29	  } catch {
    30	    return { class: null, confidence: null };
    31	  }
    32	}
    33	
    34	/**
    35	 * Effective intake: the sealed bundle already records who handed off and why,
    36	 * so the 'unknown'/'unspecified' CLI sentinels default from it (audit finding:
    37	 * the documented resume command rendered "Handed off from unknown" straight
    38	 * after sealing a real origin/reason). Explicit intake always wins. Pure and
    39	 * bundle-deterministic, so prepare and commit derive identical tokens.
    40	 * @param {any} bundle @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @returns {ReceiveOpts}
    41	 */
    42	function effectiveIntake(bundle, opts) {
    43	  const originSentinel = opts.origin === 'unknown' || opts.origin === undefined;
    44	  const reasonSentinel = opts.reason === 'unspecified' || opts.reason === undefined;
    45	  const sealedOrigin = typeof bundle?.origin?.platform === 'string' && bundle.origin.platform !== 'unknown' ? bundle.origin.platform : null;
    46	  const sealedReason = typeof bundle?.handoff?.reason === 'string' && bundle.handoff.reason.length > 0 ? bundle.handoff.reason : null;
    47	  return {
    48	    ...opts,
    49	    origin: originSentinel && sealedOrigin ? sealedOrigin : opts.origin,
    50	    reason: reasonSentinel && sealedReason ? sealedReason : opts.reason,
    51	  };
    52	}
    53	
    54	// The bound token inputs, in the fixed order the structured token serializes
    55	// them. The human names power drift diagnosis: a stale-token rejection names
    56	// exactly which input(s) moved instead of listing six guesses.
    57	const TOKEN_FIELDS = /** @type {const} */ ([
    58	  ['generation', 'bundle generation'],
    59	  ['journalSeq', 'journal seq'],
    60	  ['handoffStatus', 'handoff status'],
    61	  ['receives', 'receive count'],
    62	  ['origin', 'intake origin'],
    63	  ['reason', 'intake reason'],
    64	  ['reasonClass', 'intake reason-class'],
    65	  ['configDigest', 'config'],
    66	  ['gitDigest', 'git state'],
    67	  ['probesDigest', 'probe cache'],
    68	  ['platform', 'target platform'],
    69	  ['sessionHint', 'target session'],
    70	]);
    71	const TOKEN_PREFIX = 'rcpt1';
    72	
    73	/**
    74	 * The revision fingerprint the receipt token derives from. Every field is a
    75	 * bound input: any drift between prepare and commit re-derives a different
    76	 * token, so a stale receipt can never commit (plan §Concurrency).
    77	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
    78	 * @returns {Record<string, any>}
    79	 */
    80	function tokenInputs(root, bundle, opts, io) {
    81	  let configDigest = 'absent';
    82	  try {
    83	    configDigest = dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(`${root}/baton.config.json`, 'utf8'));
    84	  } catch {
    85	    // no config — 'absent' is itself a bound value
    86	  }
    87	  return {
    88	    generation: bundle.generation,
    89	    journalSeq: bundle.journalSeq,
    90	    // The seal state is a bound input too (gate-2 fix): a competing receiver
    91	    // that lands between this token's derivation and its commit changes
    92	    // status/receive_log, so the loser's token cannot re-derive.
    93	    handoffStatus: bundle.handoff?.status ?? null,
    94	    receives: Array.isArray(bundle.handoff?.receive_log) ? bundle.handoff.receive_log.length : 0,
    95	    origin: opts.origin,
    96	    reason: opts.reason,
    97	    // Explicit --reason-class is a bound intake input (gate-2 iter-2 M4): it
    98	    // changes the avoid[] decision, so a change between prepare and commit is
    99	    // drift that must re-prepare.
   100	    reasonClass: opts.reasonClass ?? null,
   101	    configDigest,
   102	    gitDigest: dedupeKey(opts.gitSnapshot ?? null),
   103	    probesDigest: dedupeKey(opts.probes ?? null),
   104	    platform: opts.platform,
   105	    sessionHint: opts.sessionHint,
   106	  };
   107	}
   108	
   109	/**
   110	 * Structured receipt token: a fixed-order join of per-input digests. Equality
   111	 * still gates commit exactly as the old opaque hash did; the structure only
   112	 * buys DIAGNOSIS — a mismatch can name the drifted input(s).
   113	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
   114	 */
   115	function deriveToken(root, bundle, opts, io) {
   116	  const inputs = tokenInputs(root, bundle, opts, io);
   117	  return `${TOKEN_PREFIX}.` + TOKEN_FIELDS.map(([key]) => dedupeKey(inputs[key] ?? null).slice(0, 10)).join('.');
   118	}
   119	
   120	/**
   121	 * Name the inputs whose digests differ between two structured tokens.
   122	 * @param {string} presented @param {string} expected @returns {string[]}
   123	 */
   124	function driftedInputs(presented, expected) {
   125	  const a = presented.split('.');
   126	  const b = expected.split('.');
   127	  if (a[0] !== TOKEN_PREFIX || b[0] !== TOKEN_PREFIX || a.length !== b.length) return [];
   128	  return TOKEN_FIELDS.filter((_, i) => a[i + 1] !== b[i + 1]).map(([, name]) => name);
   129	}
   130	
   131	/**
   132	 * @param {any} bundle @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
   133	 * @returns {string[]} staleness/degradation warnings
   134	 */
   135	function stalenessWarnings(bundle, opts, io) {
   136	  /** @type {string[]} */
   137	  const warnings = [];
   138	  if (bundle.handoff.status === 'open') {
   139	    warnings.push('source bundle is unsealed (open) — the handoff was never finalized; treating this as a degraded (limit-death) receive');
   140	  }
   141	  const ref = bundle.handoff.finalizedAt ?? bundle.updatedAt;
   142	  const ageMs = Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(ref);
   143	  if (Number.isFinite(ageMs) && ageMs > STALE_AFTER_MS) {
   144	    warnings.push(`bundle finalized ${Math.round(ageMs / 3_600_000)}h ago (staleness threshold 12h) — re-verify before continuing`);
   145	  }
   146	  const g = opts.gitSnapshot;
   147	  if (g && bundle.git) {
   148	    if (bundle.git.headSha !== g.headSha) {
   149	      warnings.push(`HEAD moved since capture: bundle recorded ${bundle.git.headSha}, working tree now ${g.headSha}`);
   150	    }
   151	    if (bundle.git.dirty !== g.dirty) {
   152	      warnings.push(`dirty-state mismatch: bundle captured dirty=${bundle.git.dirty}, working tree now dirty=${g.dirty}`);
   153	    }
   154	  }
   155	  return warnings;
   156	}
   157	
   158	/**
   159	 * Phase 1 of the receive transaction: validate, resolve roles, render the
   160	 * resume prompt, and return a one-time receipt token. Mutates NOTHING — the
   161	 * token is self-contained (a fingerprint hash), never a persisted record.
   162	 * @param {string} root @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
   163	 * @returns {{token: string, prompt: string, assignments: Record<string, any>, warnings: string[]}}
   164	 */
   165	export function prepare(root, opts, io) {
   166	  const { bundle, warnings: loadWarnings } = loadBundle(root, io);
   167	  if (bundle === null) throw new Error(`no handoff bundle under ${root}/.handoff — nothing to receive`);
   168	  opts = effectiveIntake(bundle, opts);
   169	
   170	  const warnings = [...loadWarnings, ...stalenessWarnings(bundle, opts, io)];
   171	
   172	  const { config, errors } = loadConfig(root, io);
   173	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
   174	  let assignments = {};
   175	  if (config) {
   176	    // The dead origin comes from the BUNDLE first (its sealed reasonClass and
   177	    // recorded origin platform); typed intake only fills the gaps — a user
   178	    // should not need to retype the verbatim limit string to keep roles off
   179	    // the platform that died (gate-2 reviewer-b finding 4). Confidence gates
   180	    // auto-avoidance (gate-2 iter-2 M4): an explicit --reason-class or a sealed
   181	    // reasonClass is trusted; a live text classification only auto-avoids at
   182	    // medium+ confidence, else it warns and defers to explicit intake.
   183	    let reasonClass;
   184	    let confidence;
   185	    if (typeof opts.reasonClass === 'string') {
   186	      reasonClass = opts.reasonClass;
   187	      confidence = 'high'; // explicit user intake
   188	    } else if (typeof bundle.handoff?.reasonClass === 'string') {
   189	      reasonClass = bundle.handoff.reasonClass;
   190	      confidence = 'high'; // trusted seal
   191	    } else {
   192	      const v = classifyReason(opts.reason, opts.origin, io);
   193	      reasonClass = v.class;
   194	      confidence = v.confidence;
   195	    }
   196	    const deadOrigin =
   197	      typeof bundle.origin?.platform === 'string' && bundle.origin.platform !== 'unknown' ? bundle.origin.platform : opts.origin;
   198	    /** @type {string[]} */
   199	    let avoid = [];
   200	    if (reasonClass === 'usage-limit') {
   201	      if (confidence === 'low') {
   202	        warnings.push(
   203	          `the switch reason matched a usage-limit heuristic only at LOW confidence — not auto-avoiding ${deadOrigin}; pass --reason-class usage-limit to confirm the failover away from it`,
   204	        );
   205	      } else {
   206	        avoid = [deadOrigin];
   207	      }
   208	    }
   209	    assignments = resolveRoles({ config, to: opts.platform, avoid, nativeOnly: false, probes: opts.probes ?? null }).assignments;
   210	  } else {
   211	    warnings.push(`role matrix unavailable (${errors.map((e) => e.msg).join('; ')}) — no role table in this prompt`);
   212	  }
   213	
   214	  const prompt = renderResumePrompt({ bundle, assignments, warnings, origin: opts.origin, reason: opts.reason });
   215	  return { token: deriveToken(root, bundle, opts, io), prompt, assignments, warnings };
   216	}
   217	
   218	/**
   219	 * Phase 2: one atomic transition. Re-derives every bound input from current
   220	 * disk state + opts and rejects ANY drift (throws, zero mutation). On match:
   221	 * writes the received seal, archives it via a 'receive' rotation, then opens a
   222	 * fresh writable generation owned by the receiving platform/session.
   223	 * @param {string} root @param {string} token @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
   224	 * @returns {{adopted: boolean, generation: number, archivedTo: string | null, alreadyCommitted?: boolean}}
   225	 */
   226	export function commit(root, token, opts, io) {
   227	  // ONE operation-scoped lock across revalidation and every mutation (gate-2
   228	  // fix: reviewers a#1/b#11): two competing receivers serialize here, and the
   229	  // loser's re-derivation sees the winner's generation/status/receive_log.
   230	  return withLock(root, io, (fence) => commitLocked(root, token, opts, io, fence));
   231	}
   232	
   233	/**
   234	 * @param {string} root @param {string} token @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io @param {string} fence
   235	 * @returns {{adopted: boolean, generation: number, archivedTo: string | null, alreadyCommitted?: boolean}}
   236	 */
   237	function commitLocked(root, token, opts, io, fence) {
   238	  const { bundle } = loadBundle(root, io);
   239	  if (bundle === null) throw new Error(`no handoff bundle under ${root}/.handoff — nothing to receive; run receive --prepare first`);
   240	  opts = effectiveIntake(bundle, opts);
   241	
   242	  const expected = deriveToken(root, bundle, opts, io);
   243	  if (expected !== token) {
   244	    // Idempotency first (audit finding: duplicate-receive corruption): if THIS
   245	    // exact receipt already landed — its digest is recorded on a receive_log
   246	    // entry — the retry is a lost-output re-send, not drift. Report success
   247	    // with zero mutation instead of sending the model into a re-prepare +
   248	    // re-commit loop that executes a second full receive.
   249	    const receipt = dedupeKey(token).slice(0, 16);
   250	    const landed = Array.isArray(bundle.handoff?.receive_log) && bundle.handoff.receive_log.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => e?.receipt === receipt);
   251	    if (landed) {
   252	      return { adopted: false, alreadyCommitted: true, generation: bundle.generation, archivedTo: null };
   253	    }
   254	    const drifted = driftedInputs(token, expected);
   255	    const detail = drifted.length > 0 ? `drifted input(s): ${drifted.join(', ')}` : 'a bound input drifted since prepare';
   256	    throw new Error(
   257	      `receipt token is stale — ${detail}. Re-prepare and retry, passing the SAME intake flags (--origin/--reason/--reason-class) to both prepare and --commit`,
   258	    );
   259	  }
   260	
   261	  const degraded = bundle.handoff.status !== 'sealed';
   262	  const receiveEntry = {
   263	    origin: opts.origin,
   264	    reason: opts.reason,
   265	    at: io.now(),
   266	    // The receipt digest keys the idempotent-retry detection above.
   267	    receipt: dedupeKey(token).slice(0, 16),
   268	    ...(degraded ? { degradedSeal: true } : {}),
   269	  };
   270	
   271	  // The degraded seal must NOT persist a LOW-confidence classification (iter-3
   272	  // F10): a sealed reasonClass is trusted as high-confidence by the next
   273	  // prepare, so laundering a low-confidence heuristic here would silently
   274	  // auto-avoid the origin one hop later. Explicit intake is high-confidence and
   275	  // seals; a live classification seals ONLY at medium+ confidence, else null
   276	  // (the next receive re-classifies the reason text at its true confidence).
   277	  let sealedReasonClass = null;
   278	  if (degraded) {
   279	    if (typeof opts.reasonClass === 'string') {
   280	      sealedReasonClass = opts.reasonClass;

     8	 * Read-only: prints assignments, never mutates the bundle (receive owns that).
     9	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    10	 * @returns {number}

    12	export function cmdRemap(args, io) {

    37	    // rate-limited platforms are skipped like avoid[] entries; a stale or

     6	/**
     7	 * `baton receive` — the two-phase resume flow. `--print-prompt` and
     8	 * `--prepare` are read-only (prepare mutates nothing); `--commit <token>`
     9	 * performs the atomic transition. The receiving session hint defaults to a
    10	 * host-stable value so prepare and commit issued by separate CLI processes
    11	 * still derive the same token; adapters pass `--session` with their real id.
    12	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    13	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    14	 */
    15	export async function cmdReceive(args, io) {
    16	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { platform: 'string', origin: 'string', reason: 'string', 'reason-class': 'string', session: 'string', commit: 'string', prepare: 'boolean', 'print-prompt': 'boolean' });
    17	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'receive', parsed.error);
    18	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    19	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;
    20	  if (!platform) return usageError(io, flags, 'receive', '--platform <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
    21	  const pErr = platformError(platform);
    22	  if (pErr) return usageError(io, flags, 'receive', pErr);
    23	
    24	  // Validate --reason-class against the class vocabulary BEFORE it is trusted as
    25	  // explicit intake and bound into the receipt token (iter-3 F11): finalize
    26	  // already does this, so receive must too — an out-of-enum class would seal a
    27	  // meaningless reasonClass verbatim.
    28	  const REASON_CLASSES = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
    29	  if (flags['reason-class'] !== undefined && !REASON_CLASSES.includes(/** @type {string} */ (flags['reason-class']))) {
    30	    return usageError(io, flags, 'receive', `--reason-class must be one of ${REASON_CLASSES.join('|')}`);
    31	  }
    32	
    33	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    34	  const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
    35	  const hasSession = typeof flags.session === 'string';
    36	  const opts = {
    37	    platform,
    38	    origin: typeof flags.origin === 'string' ? flags.origin : 'unknown',
    39	    reason: typeof flags.reason === 'string' ? flags.reason : 'unspecified',
    40	    gitSnapshot: git,
    41	    // The fresh probe cache is a bound token input (probesDigest): remap uses
    42	    // it at prepare, and a cache change before commit is drift → re-prepare.
    43	    probes: readProbeCache(root, io),
    44	    // Explicit reason-class intake (gate-2 iter-2 M4): confirms a failover away
    45	    // from a low-confidence-classified origin; bound into the token.
    46	    ...(typeof flags['reason-class'] === 'string' ? { reasonClass: flags['reason-class'] } : {}),
    47	    sessionHint: hasSession ? /** @type {string} */ (flags.session) : `cli:${io.host}`,
    48	    // A defaulted host-derived hint is NOT the harness session id — mark it
    49	    // unstable so the receiving session's first real hook checkpoint adopts
    50	    // ownership instead of being rejected as foreign (gate-2 fix 2).
    51	    sessionUnstable: !hasSession,
    52	  };
    53	
    54	  // A lost commit token (empty shell var, --commit followed by another flag)
    55	  // must be a loud usage error — falling through to the read-only prepare path
    56	  // with exit 0 convinced models a commit landed when nothing mutated.
    57	  if (flags.commit !== undefined && typeof flags.commit !== 'string') {
    58	    return usageError(io, flags, 'receive', '--commit requires the receipt token printed by prepare (the token argument is missing)');
    59	  }
    60	  if (flags.commit !== undefined && flags.prepare !== undefined) {
    61	    return usageError(io, flags, 'receive', '--prepare and --commit are mutually exclusive phases');
    62	  }
    63	
    64	  if (typeof flags.commit === 'string') {
    65	    try {
    66	      const res = commit(root, flags.commit, opts, io);
    67	      if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: res });
    68	      else if (res.alreadyCommitted === true) io.stdout.write(`already committed — generation ${res.generation} is open on ${platform}; nothing to redo\n`);
    69	      else io.stdout.write(`received — generation ${res.generation} open on ${platform}; archived ${res.archivedTo}\n`);
    70	      return 0;
    71	    } catch (err) {
    72	      const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
    73	      if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'commit-rejected', msg } });
    74	      else io.stderr.write(`baton receive: ${msg}\n`);
    75	      return 1;
    76	    }
    77	  }
    78	
    79	  /** @type {ReturnType<typeof prepare>} */
    80	  let prepared;
    81	  try {
    82	    prepared = prepare(root, opts, io);
    83	  } catch (err) {
    84	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
    85	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'prepare-failed', msg } });
    86	    else io.stderr.write(`baton receive: ${msg}\n`);
    87	    return 1;
    88	  }
    89	
    90	  if (flags['print-prompt'] === true) {

    92	    // form stays raw markdown — the adapter hook fallback path is unchanged.
    93	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { prompt: prepared.prompt, warnings: prepared.warnings } });
    94	    else io.stdout.write(prepared.prompt);
    95	    return 0;
    96	  }
    97

    99	    emitEnvelope(io, {
   100	      ok: true,
   101	      data: { token: prepared.token, prompt: prepared.prompt, assignments: prepared.assignments, warnings: prepared.warnings },
   102	    });
   103	  } else {
   104	    for (const w of prepared.warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton receive: ${w}\n`);

   106	    io.stdout.write(prepared.prompt);
   107	  }
   108	  return 0;

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba core/src/roles/resolve.mjs | sed -n '1,280p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Pure, explainable role resolution over the committed matrix. Every decision
     3	 * is auditable: chosen entries carry their chainIndex; passed-over entries land
     4	 * in skipped[] with a why. avoid[] population is the caller's job.
     5	 * @param {{config: any, to: string, avoid?: string[], nativeOnly?: boolean, probes?: Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}} input
     6	 * @returns {{assignments: Record<string, any>, notes: string[]}}
     7	 */
     8	export function resolveRoles({ config, to, avoid = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null }) {
     9	  /** @type {string[]} */
    10	  const notes = [];
    11	
    12	  /**
    13	   * The eligibility verdict for a platform, or null when eligible.
    14	   * @param {string} platform @returns {string | null}
    15	   */
    16	  function skipReason(platform) {
    17	    if (avoid.includes(platform)) return 'avoided';
    18	    if (!(platform in (config.platforms ?? {}))) return 'unknown-platform';
    19	    if (config.platforms[platform]?.enabled === false) return 'disabled';
    20	    if (probes && probes[platform]) {
    21	      const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    22	      if (outcome === 'rate-limited') return 'rate-limited';
    23	      if (capability === 'not-installed') return 'not-installed';
    24	      // 'installed' means installation was PROVEN but the probe was capped there
    25	      // (capOnSuccess) — authentication is UNVERIFIED, not verified-failed. Only
    26	      // a probe that ran and did NOT succeed (outcome !== 'ok') is a verified
    27	      // auth/reachability failure worth skipping; a capped success stays
    28	      // selectable and is flagged degraded below (iter-3 F1, plan §Role matrix).
    29	      if (capability === 'installed' && outcome !== 'ok') return 'unauthenticated';
    30	    }
    31	    return null;
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  // A selection is degraded when its availability is UNVERIFIED (gate-2 iter-2
    35	  // M3, iter-3 F1): with no probe cache at all, resolution is offline and EVERY
    36	  // selection is degraded (plan: "Offline resolution always succeeds, flagged
    37	  // degraded"); with a partial cache, platforms the cache is missing are; and a
    38	  // platform probed only to 'installed' (auth unverified, e.g. a --version
    39	  // success) is degraded even though it stays selectable.
    40	  /** @param {string} platform */
    41	  const probeUnverified = (platform) => {
    42	    if (probes == null || !probes[platform]) return true;
    43	    const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    44	    // Degraded when ANY required dimension is unverified: a capped 'installed'
    45	    // (auth never confirmed) OR a non-ok outcome (iter-4 I6) — a selectable
    46	    // platform whose last probe ERRORED or TIMED OUT is presented as
    47	    // reachability-verified otherwise, which is exactly the silent-degradation
    48	    // the plan's degraded flag exists to surface. (rate-limited is skipped, not
    49	    // selected, so it never reaches here.)
    50	    return capability === 'installed' || outcome !== 'ok';
    51	  };
    52	
    53	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    54	  const assignments = {};
    55	  for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(config.roles)) {
    56	    /** @type {any[]} */
    57	    const skipped = [];
    58	    /** @type {any} */
    59	    let assignment = null;
    60	
    61	    for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    62	      const entry = chain[i];
    63	      if (nativeOnly && entry.platform !== to) {
    64	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'non-native' });
    65	        continue;
    66	      }
    67	      const why = skipReason(entry.platform);
    68	      if (why) {
    69	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why });
    70	        continue;
    71	      }
    72	      assignment = {
    73	        platform: entry.platform,
    74	        model: entry.model,
    75	        effort: entry.effort ?? null,
    76	        mode: entry.platform === to ? 'native' : 'delegated',
    77	        chainIndex: i,
    78	        skipped,
    79	      };
    80	      if (probeUnverified(entry.platform)) assignment.degraded = true;
    81	      break;
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    if (!assignment && nativeOnly) {
    85	      // Forced-default is only valid when the destination itself is eligible.
    86	      const whyTo = skipReason(to);
    87	      const model = config.defaults?.[to];
    88	      if (!whyTo && typeof model === 'string') {
    89	        assignment = { platform: to, model, effort: null, mode: 'forced-default', chainIndex: null, skipped };
    90	        if (probeUnverified(to)) assignment.degraded = true;
    91	      }
    92	    }
    93	
    94	    if (!assignment) {
    95	      assignment = { platform: null, model: null, effort: null, mode: 'unavailable', chainIndex: null, skipped };
    96	    }
    97	    assignments[role] = assignment;
    98	  }
    99	
   100	  return { assignments, notes };
   101	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Load + validate baton.config.json, parsing role chains from their
     3	 * "platform/model[@effort]" string form into structured entries.
     4	 * Never throws: returns {config, errors} with dotted-path errors.
     5	 * @param {string} cwd @param {any} io
     6	 * @returns {{config: any | null, errors: {path: string, msg: string}[]}}
     7	 */
     8	export function loadConfig(cwd, io) {
     9	  const file = `${cwd}/baton.config.json`;
    10	  /** @type {{path: string, msg: string}[]} */
    11	  const errors = [];
    12	
    13	  let raw;
    14	  try {
    15	    raw = io.fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
    16	  } catch {
    17	    return { config: null, errors: [{ path: '', msg: `baton.config.json not found at ${file} — run 'baton init' to scaffold it` }] };
    18	  }
    19	  let parsed;
    20	  try {
    21	    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    22	  } catch (err) {
    23	    return { config: null, errors: [{ path: '', msg: `baton.config.json is not valid JSON (${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? 'parse error'})` }] };
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  if (parsed.roles === null || typeof parsed.roles !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed.roles)) {
    27	    errors.push({ path: 'roles', msg: 'roles must be an object mapping role names to chain arrays' });
    28	  }
    29	
    30	  /** @type {Record<string, any[]>} */
    31	  const roles = {};
    32	  if (errors.length === 0) {
    33	    for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(parsed.roles)) {
    34	      if (!Array.isArray(chain)) {
    35	        errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}`, msg: 'a role chain must be an array of "platform/model[@effort]" strings' });
    36	        continue;
    37	      }
    38	      /** @type {any[]} */
    39	      const entries = [];
    40	      for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    41	        const s = chain[i];
    42	        const slash = typeof s === 'string' ? s.indexOf('/') : -1;
    43	        if (slash <= 0 || slash === s.length - 1) {
    44	          errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}[${i}]`, msg: `malformed chain entry ${JSON.stringify(s)} — expected "platform/model[@effort]"` });
    45	          continue;
    46	        }
    47	        const platform = s.slice(0, slash);
    48	        const rest = s.slice(slash + 1);
    49	        const at = rest.indexOf('@');
    50	        const model = at === -1 ? rest : rest.slice(0, at);
    51	        const effort = at === -1 ? null : rest.slice(at + 1);
    52	        if (parsed.platforms && !(platform in parsed.platforms)) {
    53	          errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}[${i}]`, msg: `unknown platform "${platform}" — not a key of config.platforms` });
    54	          continue;
    55	        }
    56	        entries.push({ platform, model, effort });
    57	      }
    58	      roles[role] = entries;
    59	    }
    60	  }
    61	
    62	  if (errors.length > 0) return { config: null, errors };
    63	  return { config: { ...parsed, roles }, errors };
    64	}

     1	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     2	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
     3	import { bundlePaths, loadBundle, writeSnapshotIn, rotateJournalIn } from '../bundle/store.mjs';
     4	import { withLock } from '../bundle/lock.mjs';
     5	import { snapshot as gitSnapshot } from '../git/snapshot.mjs';
     6	import { loadSignatures } from '../detect/signatures.mjs';
     7	import { classify } from '../detect/classifier.mjs';
     8	import { emitEnvelope, resolveRoot, usageError, parseFlagsStrict, platformError } from './shared.mjs';
     9	
    10	const BUILTIN_SIGNATURES = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
    11	
    12	/**
    13	 * Infer the reason class by running the reason text through the classifier
    14	 * against the bundle's origin platform. No signature match reads as 'ok',
    15	 * which is not a switch reason — degrade to null rather than mislabel.
    16	 * @param {string} reason @param {string} platform @param {any} io
    17	 * @returns {string | null}
    18	 */
    19	function inferReasonClass(reason, platform, io) {
    20	  try {
    21	    const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
    22	    const verdict = classify({ text: reason, exitCode: 0, platform, table, structured: null });
    23	    return verdict.class === 'ok' ? null : verdict.class;
    24	  } catch {
    25	    return null;
    26	  }
    27	}
    28	
    29	/**
    30	 * `baton finalize` — the narrative seal: records the switch reason, refreshes
    31	 * git state, seals the bundle, and freezes this generation into history via a
    32	 * 'finalize' journal rotation.
    33	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    34	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    35	 */
    36	export async function cmdFinalize(args, io) {
    37	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { reason: 'string', 'reason-class': 'string', to: 'string' });
    38	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'finalize', parsed.error);
    39	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    40	  const reason = typeof flags.reason === 'string' ? flags.reason : null;
    41	  if (!reason) return usageError(io, flags, 'finalize', '--reason "<why you are switching>" is required');
    42	  const toErr = platformError(flags.to);
    43	  if (toErr) return usageError(io, flags, 'finalize', `--to: ${toErr}`);
    44	  const REASON_CLASSES = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
    45	  if (flags['reason-class'] !== undefined && !REASON_CLASSES.includes(/** @type {string} */ (flags['reason-class']))) {
    46	    return usageError(io, flags, 'finalize', `--reason-class must be one of ${REASON_CLASSES.join('|')}`);
    47	  }
    48	
    49	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    50	  const paths = bundlePaths(root);
    51	  const { bundle, warnings } = loadBundle(root, io);
    52	  for (const w of warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton finalize: ${w}\n`);
    53	  if (bundle === null) {
    54	    const error = { code: 'no-bundle', msg: `no handoff bundle at ${paths.snapshot}; nothing to finalize` };
    55	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error });
    56	    else io.stderr.write(`baton finalize: ${error.msg}\n`);
    57	    return 1;
    58	  }
    59	
    60	  // Capture git BEFORE the lock — it is async and must not straddle the lock.
    61	  const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
    62	  if (git === null) io.stderr.write('baton finalize: git state unavailable — sealing with git marked unavailable (not the stale prior snapshot)\n');
    63	
    64	  // ONE atomic locked span (iter-5 A2): the seal, snapshot write, and journal
    65	  // rotation run under a SINGLE lock acquisition + fence, and the bundle is
    66	  // RELOADED inside the lock. The old code loaded before the async git await
    67	  // then sealed + wrote + rotated through separate locks, so a decision appended
    68	  // during the await was sealed from a stale bundle and survived only in the
    69	  // rotated journal. Reloading inside the lock includes it in the seal.
    70	  const sealed = withLock(root, io, (token) => {
    71	    const current = loadBundle(root, io).bundle;
    72	    if (current === null) return null; // deleted concurrently between the checks
    73	    const reasonClass =
    74	      typeof flags['reason-class'] === 'string' ? flags['reason-class'] : inferReasonClass(reason, current.origin.platform, io);
    75	    const s = {
    76	      ...current,
    77	      // NEVER seal the stale prior git snapshot (iter-4 I2 sibling): a receive
    78	      // audits the seal's git against live state, so stale HEAD/dirty would emit
    79	      // false "HEAD moved"/"dirty mismatch" warnings. On refresh failure seal an
    80	      // explicit unavailable (null) git; receive re-derives it live regardless.
    81	      git,
    82	      updatedAt: io.now(),
    83	      handoff: {
    84	        ...current.handoff,
    85	        status: 'sealed',
    86	        reason,
    87	        reasonClass,
    88	        toPlatformHint: typeof flags.to === 'string' ? flags.to : null,
    89	        finalizedAt: io.now(),
    90	      },
    91	    };
    92	    writeSnapshotIn(root, s, io, token);
    93	    rotateJournalIn(root, 'finalize', io, token);
    94	    return s;
    95	  });
    96	
    97	  if (sealed === null) {
    98	    const error = { code: 'no-bundle', msg: `handoff bundle vanished during finalize at ${paths.snapshot}` };
    99	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error });
   100	    else io.stderr.write(`baton finalize: ${error.msg}\n`);
   101	    return 1;
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  // Inference only matches verbatim harness banners; a natural paraphrase
   105	  // seals reasonClass:null — and receive's role remap only auto-avoids the dead
   106	  // platform on 'usage-limit'. Surface the gap instead of silently defeating
   107	  // the failover heuristics (audit finding).
   108	  if (sealed.handoff.reasonClass === null && flags['reason-class'] === undefined) {
   109	    io.stderr.write(
   110	      'baton finalize: no reason class inferred from the reason text — if this switch was forced, re-run with --reason-class <usage-limit|throttle|auth|other-error> so receive keeps roles off the exhausted platform\n',
   111	    );
   112	  }
   113	
   114	  if (flags.json) {
   115	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { bundlePath: paths.snapshot, handoffMdPath: paths.handoffMd } });
   116	  } else {
   117	    const rc = sealed.handoff.reasonClass;
   118	    io.stdout.write(`sealed — reason: ${reason}${rc ? ` (${rc})` : ''}${sealed.handoff.toPlatformHint ? ` — next: ${sealed.handoff.toPlatformHint}` : ''}\n`);
   119	  }
   120	  return 0;
   121	}

    10	const EXIT_BY_CLASS = { ok: 0, 'usage-limit': 10, throttle: 11, auth: 12, 'other-error': 13 };
    11	
    12	/**
    13	 * `baton detect` — classify harness output / structured error types.
    14	 * Exit codes ARE the verdict (frozen contract): 0 ok · 10 usage-limit ·
    15	 * 11 throttle · 12 auth · 13 other-error · 2 usage error.
    16	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    17	 * @returns {number}
    18	 */
    19	export function cmdDetect(args, io) {

    21	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'detect', parsed.error);
    22	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    23	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;

    25

    28	    table = loadSignatures(
    29	      {
    30	        builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES,

    33	      io,
    34	    );
    35	  } catch (err) {
    36	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
    37	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-signatures', msg } });
    38	    else io.stderr.write(`baton detect: ${msg}\n`);
    39	    return 1;
    40	  }
    41	
    42	  const text = typeof flags.text === 'string' ? flags.text : typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '';
    43	  const exitCode = flags['exit-code'] !== undefined ? Number(flags['exit-code']) : 0;
    44

    50	      errorType: flags['structured-error-type'],

    64	  }

    14	
    15	/** @param {any} matcher @param {string} text */
    16	function matches(matcher, text) {
    17	  switch (matcher.kind) {
    18	    case 'substring':
    19	      return text.includes(matcher.value);
    20	    case 'regex':
    21	      return new RegExp(matcher.pattern, matcher.flags || '').test(text);
    22	    case 'json-field': {
    23	      for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    24	        let obj;
    25	        try {
    26	          obj = JSON.parse(line);
    27	        } catch {
    28	          continue;
    29	        }
    30	        const val = String(matcher.path)
    31	          .split('.')
    32	          .reduce((o, k) => (o == null ? undefined : o[k]), obj);
    33	        if (val === matcher.equals) return true;
    34	      }
    35	      return false;
    36	    }
    37	    default:
    38	      return false;
    39	  }
    40	}
    41	
    42	/**
    43	 * Classify harness output. Structured evidence first (text and exit code are
    44	 * ignored when a recognized structured signal is present); text signatures as
    45	 * the fallback tier; exit code only breaks the no-match tie — a generic nonzero
    46	 * exit is never usage-limit on its own.
    47	 * @param {{text?: string, exitCode?: number, platform: string, table: any, structured?: {kind: string, errorType: string} | null}} input
    48	 * @returns {{class: string, signatureId: string | null, confidence: string | null, resetHint: string | null, tried?: string[]}}
    49	 */
    50	export function classify({ text = '', exitCode = 0, platform, table, structured = null }) {
    51	  if (structured) {
    52	    if (structured.kind === 'stop-failure') {
    53	      const cls = STOP_FAILURE_MAP[structured.errorType] ?? 'other-error';
    54	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
    55	    }
    56	    if (structured.kind === 'exec-json') {
    57	      const cls = structured.errorType === 'usage_limit' ? 'usage-limit' : 'other-error';
    58	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
    59	    }
    60	    // Unrecognized structured kind: fall through to the text tier.
    61	  }
    62	
    63	  const stripped = text.replace(ANSI, '');
    64	  const candidates = table.signatures.filter((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === platform);
    65	  /** @type {any} */
    66	  let best = null;
    67	  for (const s of candidates) {
    68	    if (!matches(s.matcher, stripped)) continue;
    69	    if (best === null || PRECEDENCE.indexOf(s.class) < PRECEDENCE.indexOf(best.class)) best = s;
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  if (best) {
    73	    let resetHint = null;
    74	    if (typeof best.resetHint === 'string') {
    75	      const m = stripped.match(new RegExp(best.resetHint));
    76	      if (m) resetHint = m[1] ?? m[0];
    77	    }
    78	    return { class: best.class, signatureId: best.id, confidence: best.confidence, resetHint };
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  return {
    82	    class: exitCode !== 0 ? 'other-error' : 'ok',
    83	    signatureId: null,
    84	    confidence: null,
    85	    resetHint: null,
    86	  };
    87	}

    13	export function bundlePaths(root) {

    28	const tsForFile = (iso) => iso.replace(/[:.]/g, '-');

    30	/** @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p */
    31	const markerPath = (p) => `${p.dir}/rotation.marker.json`;

    33	/**
    34	 * Reconcile a leftover rotation marker (crash recovery, part of load). The
    35	 * mutations run under the repo lock, acquired WITHOUT waiting (gate-2 minor
    36	 * 16): a held lock means someone is mid-operation — reconciliation defers to
    37	 * the next unlocked load instead of writing behind their back.
    38	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings @param {string} root
    39	 */
    40	function reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root) {
    41	  const mp = markerPath(p);
    42	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return;
    43	  try {
    44	    withLock(root, { ...io, lockRetry: { attempts: 0, delayMs: 0 } }, () => {
    45	      if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return; // a competitor reconciled while we acquired
    46	      const marker = safeReadJson(io.fs, mp);
    47	      if (!marker.ok) {
    48	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    49	        warnings.push('rotation marker was unparseable — rolled the rotation back (marker cleared, journal preserved)');
    50	        return;
    51	      }
    52	      const stem = `${tsForFile(marker.value.startedAt)}.${marker.value.kind}`;
    53	      const freeze = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`;
    54	      const rotated = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`;
    55	
    56	      if (io.fs.existsSync(freeze)) {
    57	        // Roll forward: finish whichever step the crash interrupted.
    58	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal) && !io.fs.existsSync(rotated)) {
    59	          io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, rotated);
    60	        }
    61	        if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
    62	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
    63	        }
    64	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    65	        warnings.push(`completed an interrupted rotation (roll-forward of ${stem}); marker cleared`);
    66	      } else {
    67	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    68	        warnings.push(`aborted an incomplete rotation (roll-back; ${stem} freeze never landed); marker cleared, journal preserved`);
    69	      }
    70	    });
    71	  } catch (err) {
    72	    if (err instanceof LockHeldError) {
    73	      warnings.push('a rotation marker is present but the repo lock is held — reconciliation deferred to the next unlocked load');
    74	      return;
    75	    }
    76	    throw err;
    77	  }
    78	}

    80	/**
    81	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings
    82	 * @returns {any | null} a usable base bundle, or null when nothing exists
    83	 */
    84	function resolveBase(io, p, warnings) {
    85	  const snapshotExists = io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot);
    86	  if (snapshotExists) {
    87	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
    88	    if (snap.ok && validateBundle(snap.value).ok) return snap.value;
    89	    warnings.push(
    90	      snap.ok
    91	        ? 'snapshot bundle.json failed schema validation — attempting recovery'
    92	        : `snapshot bundle.json is unreadable (${snap.error}) — attempting recovery`,
    93	    );
    94	    const bak = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.bak);
    95	    if (bak.ok && validateBundle(bak.value).ok) {
    96	      warnings.push('recovered from backup bundle.json.bak; replaying journal past its journalSeq');
    97	      return bak.value;
    98	    }
    99	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.bak)) warnings.push('backup bundle.json.bak is also unusable');
   100	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   101	      warnings.push('rebuilding the bundle by replaying the whole journal over a fresh seed (recovery of last resort)');
   102	      return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
   103	    }
   104	    return null;
   105	  }
   106	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   107	    warnings.push('no snapshot present — rebuilding the bundle from the journal');
   108	    return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
   109	  }
   110	  return null;
   111	}

   113	/**
   114	 * Load the active bundle with full crash recovery. Read path — not lock-gated;
   115	 * rotation-marker reconciliation is part of load. A managed tree that fails
   116	 * the symlink/realpath jail (gate-2 fix 6) is refused outright: bundle null,
   117	 * `unsafe: true`, and the problem in warnings — callers must not treat this as
   118	 * "no bundle yet" and seed through the link.
   119	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   120	 * @returns {{bundle: any | null, warnings: string[], unsafe?: boolean}}
   121	 */
   122	export function loadBundle(root, io) {
   123	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   124	  /** @type {string[]} */
   125	  const warnings = [];
   126	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.dir)) return { bundle: null, warnings };
   127	
   128	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   129	  if (!safe.ok) return { bundle: null, warnings: [safe.problem], unsafe: true };
   130	
   131	  reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root);
   132	
   133	  // An interrupted purge is visible on every read path (gate-2 minor 16): the
   134	  // tree may still hold transcript copies until purge-transcript finishes.
   135	  if (io.fs.existsSync(`${p.dir}/purge.marker.json`)) {
   136	    warnings.push('an interrupted purge left its marker — run baton purge-transcript to finish scrubbing every retained copy');
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  let bundle = resolveBase(io, p, warnings);
   140	  if (bundle === null) return { bundle: null, warnings };
   141	
   142	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   143	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
   144	    for (const w of jw) {
   145	      warnings.push(`journal line ${w.line}: ${w.kind}${w.kind === 'seq-order' ? ` (prev ${w.prev}, got ${w.seq})` : ''}`);
   146	    }
   147	    for (const e of entries) {
   148	      // Envelope guard (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable but non-object line
   149	      // (null, number, bare string) is not an event — skip it with a warning
   150	      // rather than crash replay on `e.seq` / `e.dedupeKey`.
   151	      if (e === null || typeof e !== 'object' || Array.isArray(e)) {
   152	        warnings.push('skipped a malformed journal line (not an event envelope)');
   153	        continue;
   154	      }
   155	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq <= bundle.journalSeq) continue;
   156	      bundle = applyEvent(bundle, e);
   157	    }
   158	  }
   159	  return { bundle, warnings };
   160	}

   162	/**
   163	 * Lock-context snapshot write: caller already holds the repo lock and passes
   164	 * its fencing token; the write is fence-checked immediately before landing
   165	 * (the fast-abort layer — gate-2 fix, reviewer-b finding 12).
   166	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io @param {string} token
   167	 */
   168	export function writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token) {
   169	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   170	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   171	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   172	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   173	    // Size discipline runs in the write path itself (gate-2 fix 10): every
   174	    // persisted snapshot is within budget; identity for in-budget bundles.
   175	    const bounded = compact(bundle);
   176	    // Validate-before-backup (gate-2 major 15): a corrupt current snapshot
   177	    // must never overwrite a good .bak — the backup only rotates when the
   178	    // outgoing snapshot itself would be recoverable.
   179	    const prev = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   180	    if (prev.ok && validateBundle(prev.value).ok) {
   181	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.bak, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
   182	    }
   183	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.snapshot, JSON.stringify(bounded, null, 2) + '\n');
   184	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.handoffMd, renderHandoffMd(bounded));
   185	  });
   186	}
   187	
   188	/**
   189	 * Persist a snapshot: back up the previous good one, write atomically, render
   190	 * HANDOFF.md alongside. Mutation — runs under the repo lock.
   191	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io
   192	 */
   193	export function writeSnapshot(root, bundle, io) {
   194	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token));
   195	}

   197	/**
   198	 * Append one journal entry under the repo lock. A pre-numbered entry keeps its
   199	 * seq; a seq-less entry is allocated max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) + 1.
   200	 * @param {string} root @param {any} entry @param {any} io
   201	 * @returns {number} the entry's (possibly allocated) seq
   202	 */
   203	export function appendJournal(root, entry, io) {
   204	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   205	  return withLock(root, io, (token) =>
   206	    guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   207	      assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   208	      ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   209	      let seq = entry.seq;
   210	      let toAppend = entry;
   211	      if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
   212	        const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   213	        const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   214	        let tailSeq = 0;
   215	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   216	          for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
   217	            if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
   218	          }
   219	        }
   220	        seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
   221	        toAppend = { ...entry, seq };
   222	      }
   223	      appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, toAppend);
   224	      return seq;
   225	    }),
   226	  );
   227	}

   229	/**
   230	 * Crash-safe journal rotation (marker → freeze → rename → fresh journal →
   231	 * clear marker), with 10-per-kind history retention. Mutation — lock-gated.
   232	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io
   233	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
   234	 */
   235	export function rotateJournal(root, kind, io) {
   236	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token));
   237	}
   238	
   239	/**
   240	 * Lock-context rotation: caller holds the lock and passes its fencing token.
   241	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io @param {string} token
   242	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
   243	 */
   244	export function rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token) {
   245	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   246	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   247	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   248	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   249	    const startedAt = io.now();
   250	    const stem = `${tsForFile(startedAt)}.${kind}`;
   251	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   252	    const seq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   253	
   254	    atomicWriteJson(io.fs, markerPath(p), { kind, startedAt, seq });
   255	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.historyDir);
   256	
   257	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot)) {
   258	      // Atomic freeze (gate-2 minor 16): tmp+rename, so a crash mid-copy can
   259	      // never leave a partial history snapshot that recovery would trust.
   260	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
   261	    } else {
   262	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, '');
   263	    }
   264	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   265	      io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`);
   266	    } else {
   267	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`, '');
   268	    }
   269	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
   270	
   271	    // Retention: newest RETAIN_PER_KIND per kind (ISO-derived stems sort by time).
   272	    const freezes = io.fs
   273	      .readdirSync(p.historyDir)
   274	      .filter((/** @type {string} */ n) => n.endsWith(`.${kind}.json`))
   275	      .sort();
   276	    while (freezes.length > RETAIN_PER_KIND) {
   277	      const oldest = /** @type {string} */ (freezes.shift());
   278	      const oldStem = oldest.slice(0, -'.json'.length);
   279	      for (const suffix of ['.json', '.ndjson']) {
   280	        try {
   281	          io.fs.unlinkSync(`${p.historyDir}/${oldStem}${suffix}`);
   282	        } catch {
   283	          // already absent — retention is best-effort per file
   284	        }
   285	      }
   286	    }
   287	
   288	    io.fs.unlinkSync(markerPath(p));
   289	    return stem;
   290	  });
   291	}

     1	import { atomicWriteJson } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
     2	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
     3	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
     4	import { checkHandoffTree } from '../util/jail.mjs';
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * Operation-scoped mutation lock (plan §Concurrency, Lock model).
     8	 * Acquire = atomic non-recursive mkdir of .handoff/lock/; exclusivity comes from
     9	 * EEXIST. Ownership facts live in owner.json; logical session ownership lives in
    10	 * bundle.json, never here. Takeover only when the owner is provably dead on this
    11	 * host — every other contended state refuses, and `force` never changes that.
    12	 */
    13	
    14	export class LockHeldError extends Error {
    15	  constructor(/** @type {string} */ message) {
    16	    super(message);
    17	    this.name = 'LockHeldError';
    18	  }
    19	}
    20	
    21	export class FencingError extends Error {
    22	  constructor(/** @type {string} */ message) {
    23	    super(message);
    24	    this.name = 'FencingError';
    25	  }
    26	}
    27	
    28	/** @param {string} root */
    29	function lockPaths(root) {
    30	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
    31	  return {
    32	    dir,
    33	    lockDir: `${dir}/lock`,
    34	    owner: `${dir}/lock/owner.json`,
    35	    journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson`,
    36	  };
    37	}
    38	
    39	const ESCAPE_HATCH =
    40	  'Verify manually that no baton process still holds it, then delete .handoff/lock to clear.';
    41	
    42	/**
    43	 * Inspect the current lock state.
    44	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p
    45	 * @returns {{state: 'free'} | {state: 'torn', cause: string} | {state: 'live'|'dead'|'cross-host', owner: any}}
    46	 */
    47	function inspect(io, p) {
    48	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.lockDir)) return { state: 'free' };
    49	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.owner)) {
    50	    return { state: 'torn', cause: 'owner.json is missing (torn lock metadata)' };
    51	  }
    52	  let owner;
    53	  try {
    54	    owner = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
    55	  } catch {
    56	    return { state: 'torn', cause: 'owner.json is unparseable (torn lock metadata)' };
    57	  }
    58	  if (owner.host !== io.host) return { state: 'cross-host', owner };
    59	  if (io.processAlive(owner.pid, owner.startTime)) return { state: 'live', owner };
    60	  return { state: 'dead', owner };
    61	}
    62	
    63	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p @param {string} text */
    64	function journalNote(io, p, text) {
    65	  io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
    66	  // Allocate seq + dedupeKey like every other journal entry (gate-2 fix,
    67	  // reviewer-b finding 2): a keyless, seq-less note poisons journalSeq to NaN
    68	  // on replay and dedupes every later keyless event. We are already inside the
    69	  // lock here, so reading the tail for allocation is race-free.
    70	  let tailSeq = 0;
    71	  try {
    72	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(`${p.dir}/bundle.json`, 'utf8'));
    73	    if (typeof snap?.journalSeq === 'number') tailSeq = snap.journalSeq;
    74	  } catch {
    75	    // no snapshot yet — journal tail below still counts
    76	  }
    77	  try {
    78	    for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
    79	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
    80	    }
    81	  } catch {
    82	    // no journal yet
    83	  }
    84	  const ts = io.now();
    85	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, {
    86	    seq: tailSeq + 1,
    87	    ts,
    88	    type: 'note',
    89	    dedupeKey: dedupeKey({ ts, type: 'note', source: 'lock', text }),
    90	    writerId: ['lock', io.host, io.pid].join('-'),
    91	    source: 'lock',
    92	    payload: { text },
    93	  });
    94	}
    95	
    96	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p */
    97	function publishOwner(io, p) {
    98	  const token = io.newFencingToken();
    99	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.owner, {
   100	    host: io.host,
   101	    pid: io.pid,
   102	    startTime: io.startTime,
   103	    fencingToken: token,
   104	    acquiredAt: io.now(),
   105	    heartbeatAt: io.now(),
   106	  });
   107	  return token;
   108	}
   109	
   110	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p */
   111	function acquire(io, p) {
   112	  io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   113	  try {
   114	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   115	    return publishOwner(io, p);
   116	  } catch (err) {
   117	    if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code !== 'EEXIST') throw err;
   118	  }
   119	
   120	  const s = inspect(io, p);
   121	  switch (s.state) {
   122	    case 'free':
   123	      // Raced a release between mkdir failure and inspection; retry once. A
   124	      // competitor can win THIS mkdir too (gate-2 fix 12: observed under real
   125	      // barrier-released contention) — that is contention, not corruption.
   126	      try {
   127	        io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   128	      } catch (err) {
   129	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') {
   130	          throw new LockHeldError('lock was re-acquired by a competitor mid-inspection — transient contention');
   131	        }
   132	        throw err;
   133	      }
   134	      return publishOwner(io, p);
   135	    case 'live':
   136	      throw new LockHeldError(
   137	        `lock is held by a live process (pid ${s.owner.pid} on ${s.owner.host}) — terminate that process first.`,
   138	      );
   139	    case 'cross-host':
   140	      throw new Error(
   141	        `lock is held from another host ('${s.owner.host}', this is '${io.host}') — cross-host locks are never provably safe to steal. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
   142	      );
   143	    case 'torn':
   144	      // During ACQUIRE, torn metadata is usually a competitor caught between
   145	      // its mkdir and owner.json publication — transient by construction, so
   146	      // LockHeldError lets withLock wait it out (gate-2 fix 12). A genuinely
   147	      // crashed publisher exhausts the retry budget and surfaces this same
   148	      // message; recovery semantics (recoverLock) are unchanged: never steal.
   149	      throw new LockHeldError(`${s.cause} — unsupported for automatic recovery. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`);
   150	    case 'dead': {
   151	      // Atomic dead-lock reclaim (gate-2 iter-2 B1, hardened iter-3 finding-1).
   152	      // rm-then-mkdir is NOT mutually exclusive: two contenders can both observe
   153	      // 'dead' before either removes the stale dir, then both rm+mkdir — the
   154	      // second silently clobbers the first, yielding two simultaneous holders and
   155	      // two takeover notes. rename() is the atomic arbiter instead: the stale
   156	      // lock dir has exactly one name, so only one process can move it aside (the
   157	      // rest get ENOENT). Winning the rename IS winning the reclaim; a contender
   158	      // that recreated the dir via the 'free' path first makes our mkdir EEXIST →
   159	      // transient contention. The fencing token stays the write-time backstop.
   160	      const aside = `${p.lockDir}.reclaim-${io.pid}-${io.newFencingToken()}`;
   161	      try {
   162	        io.fs.renameSync(p.lockDir, aside);
   163	      } catch (err) {
   164	        // Source already gone → another contender is reclaiming it; wait+retry.
   165	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'ENOENT') {
   166	          throw new LockHeldError('a competitor is reclaiming the dead lock — transient contention');
   167	        }
   168	        throw err;
   169	      }
   170	      io.fs.rmSync(aside, { recursive: true, force: true });
   171	      try {
   172	        io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   173	      } catch (err) {
   174	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') {
   175	          throw new LockHeldError('a competitor re-claimed the dead lock first — transient contention');
   176	        }
   177	        throw err;
   178	      }
   179	      const token = publishOwner(io, p);
   180	      // Journal the takeover only now — we HOLD the lock (fresh dir + published
   181	      // owner), so the note's seq is allocated while holding it (plan
   182	      // §Concurrency "sequence numbers are allocated under the lock"), never
   183	      // racing a free-path competitor's concurrent append (iter-3 F2). The note
   184	      // is best-effort (0-or-1) and MUST NOT abort acquisition (iter-4 I7): if it
   185	      // threw here — after publishOwner, before the token is returned to withLock
   186	      // — the release finally would never run and the lock would leak with a live
   187	      // owner. Swallow it; the reclaim already succeeded.
   188	      try {
   189	        journalNote(io, p, `lock takeover: took over stale lock from dead pid ${s.owner.pid} (start time ${s.owner.startTime})`);
   190	      } catch {
   191	        // audit note is best-effort
   192	      }
   193	      return token;
   194	    }
   195	    /* c8 ignore next 2 */
   196	    default:
   197	      throw new Error('unreachable lock state');
   198	  }
   199	}
   200	
   201	/**
   202	 * Fence-guarded release: the lock dir is removed only while owner.json still
   203	 * carries OUR token. A holder that paused past a takeover must not delete the
   204	 * competitor's lock on its way out — release is a write like any other.
   205	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p @param {string} token
   206	 */
   207	function release(io, p, token) {
   208	  try {
   209	    const current = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
   210	    if (current.fencingToken !== token) return; // a competitor took over — their lock, not ours
   211	  } catch {
   212	    return; // owner metadata gone or torn mid-hold — never guess; leave it for recovery
   213	  }
   214	  io.fs.rmSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   215	}
   216	
   217	// A live same-host holder is transient by design — operation-scoped locks are
   218	// held for milliseconds — so contention waits instead of dropping the
   219	// operation (gate-2 fix 12: simultaneous checkpoints must all land). The budget
   220	// is generous (≈15 s): under heavy load a writer may be starved of scheduling
   221	// for seconds, and waiting is strictly safer than silently dropping its work.
   222	// Every other contended state (torn, cross-host) stays fail-fast: waiting
   223	// cannot make those provably safe.
   224	const DEFAULT_RETRY = { attempts: 600, delayMs: 25 };
   225	
   226	/** Synchronous sleep without burning a core: Atomics.wait on a throwaway buffer. */
   227	const sleepMs = (/** @type {number} */ ms) => {
   228	  Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, ms);
   229	};
   230	
   231	/**
   232	 * Acquire → run fn(token) → release (also on throw). Synchronous by design:
   233	 * everything the lock guards is sync fs work. Live-held locks are retried on
   234	 * a bounded budget (override via io.lockRetry = {attempts, delayMs}).
   235	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {(token: string) => any} fn
   236	 */
   237	export function withLock(root, io, fn) {
   238	  const p = lockPaths(root);
   239	  // Jail BEFORE any lock mutation (gate-2 iter-2 B2): a symlinked .handoff or
   240	  // .handoff/lock must never be created into or removed through — that would
   241	  // reach outside the repository. Checked once here, ahead of acquire.
   242	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   243	  if (!safe.ok) throw new Error(`${safe.problem} — refusing to acquire the lock`);
   244	  const retry = io.lockRetry ?? DEFAULT_RETRY;
   245	  /** @type {string} */
   246	  let token;
   247	  for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
   248	    try {
   249	      token = acquire(io, p);
   250	      break;
   251	    } catch (err) {
   252	      if (err instanceof LockHeldError && attempt < retry.attempts) {
   253	        sleepMs(retry.delayMs);
   254	        continue;
   255	      }
   256	      throw err;
   257	    }
   258	  }
   259	  try {
   260	    return fn(token);
   261	  } finally {
   262	    release(io, p, token);
   263	  }
   264	}
   265	
   266	/**
   267	 * Fast-abort layer: re-read the published token immediately before a write; a
   268	 * holder that paused past its last check aborts instead of clobbering.
   269	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {string} token @param {() => any} fn
   270	 */
   271	export function guardedWrite(root, io, token, fn) {
   272	  const p = lockPaths(root);
   273	  let current = null;
   274	  try {
   275	    current = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
   276	  } catch {
   277	    throw new FencingError('lock owner metadata is gone — fencing check failed; aborting write');
   278	  }
   279	  if (current.fencingToken !== token) {
   280	    throw new FencingError(
   281	      `fencing token changed (held '${token}', current '${current.fencingToken}') — another process took over; aborting write`,
   282	    );
   283	  }
   284	  return fn();
   285	}
   286	
   287	/**
   288	 * Explicit recovery. `force` is an intent flag only — it never changes the
   289	 * decision: exactly one state recovers (provably-dead same-host owner).
   290	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {{force?: boolean}} [opts]
   291	 * @returns {{recovered: boolean, refusedReason: string | null}}
   292	 */
   293	export function recoverLock(root, io, opts = {}) {
   294	  void opts;
   295	  const p = lockPaths(root);
   296	  // Jail before recovery (gate-2 iter-2 B2): recoverLock removes the lock dir,
   297	  // so a symlinked managed tree must be refused first — even with force, a
   298	  // symlink is never provably safe to delete through.
   299	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   300	  if (!safe.ok) return { recovered: false, refusedReason: `${safe.problem} — refusing recovery` };
   301	  const s = inspect(io, p);
   302	  switch (s.state) {
   303	    case 'free':
   304	      return { recovered: true, refusedReason: null };
   305	    case 'dead': {
   306	      // Reclaim atomically via a single acquire() attempt + release, NOT an
   307	      // inspect-then-rmSync (iter-4 I1): the old bare rmSync had a TOCTOU — a
   308	      // competitor could win the rename-arbitrated dead-reclaim between this
   309	      // inspect() and the rmSync, publish a LIVE owner, and recovery would then
   310	      // delete a lock whose owner is alive. acquire() re-inspects atomically and
   311	      // shares the SAME rename arbitration, so recovery and a concurrent takeover
   312	      // are mutually exclusive: acquire either reclaims a still-dead lock (and
   313	      // journals the takeover under the lock) or throws because it is now
   314	      // live/torn — in which case we refuse rather than steal. release() then
   315	      // leaves the slot clear.
   316	      let token;
   317	      try {
   318	        token = acquire(io, p);
   319	      } catch (err) {
   320	        return { recovered: false, refusedReason: /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err) };
   321	      }
   322	      release(io, p, token);
   323	      return { recovered: true, refusedReason: null };
   324	    }
   325	    case 'live':
   326	      return {
   327	        recovered: false,
   328	        refusedReason: `lock is held by a live process (pid ${s.owner.pid} on ${s.owner.host}) — terminate that process first.`,
   329	      };
   330	    case 'cross-host':
   331	      return {
   332	        recovered: false,
   333	        refusedReason: `lock is held from another host ('${s.owner.host}', this is '${io.host}') — cross-host locks are never provably safe to steal, even with force. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
   334	      };
   335	    case 'torn':
   336	      return {
   337	        recovered: false,
   338	        refusedReason: `${s.cause} — unsupported for automatic recovery, even with force. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
   339	      };
   340	    /* c8 ignore next 2 */
   341	    default:
   342	      return { recovered: false, refusedReason: 'unreachable lock state' };
   343	  }
   344	}

     1	import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
     2	
     3	// Git facts degrade to null rather than block a checkpoint: not-a-repo,
     4	// missing binary, and timeouts are all expected environments (plan §Root
     5	// discovery & degraded modes).
     6	const GIT_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000;
     7	// The untracked digest is bounded: beyond this many paths, entries fold in by
     8	// name only, keeping prepare-time hashing cheap on huge untracked trees.
     9	const UNTRACKED_CAP = 200;
    10	// NUL separator: cannot occur in diffs, sizes, hex hashes, or file paths, so
    11	// joined digest inputs cannot collide across field boundaries.
    12	const SEP = String.fromCharCode(0);
    13	const NEWLINE = String.fromCharCode(10);
    14	
    15	const sha256 = (/** @type {string} */ s) => createHash('sha256').update(s).digest('hex');
    16	
    17	/**
    18	 * Capture the repo's git state plus a content-sensitive digest. The digest
    19	 * covers the staged diff, unstaged diff, and a bounded untracked-files digest
    20	 * (path + size + content hash) — NOT the HEAD sha, which the receive token
    21	 * binds as its own input, so a HEAD-only move is distinguishable from a
    22	 * content change.
    23	 * @param {{execFile: Function, cwd: string, fs: any}} deps
    24	 * @returns {Promise<{branch: string, headSha: string, dirty: boolean, dirtySummary: string[], contentDigest: string} | null>}
    25	 */
    26	export async function snapshot({ execFile, cwd, fs }) {
    27	  const git = (/** @type {string[]} */ args) => execFile('git', args, { cwd, timeout: GIT_TIMEOUT_MS });
    28	  let branch, head, status, staged, unstaged, untrackedList;
    29	  try {
    30	    [branch, head, status, staged, unstaged, untrackedList] = await Promise.all([
    31	      git(['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD']),
    32	      git(['rev-parse', 'HEAD']),
    33	      git(['status', '--porcelain']),
    34	      git(['diff', '--cached']),
    35	      git(['diff']),
    36	      git(['ls-files', '--others', '--exclude-standard']),
    37	    ]);
    38	  } catch {
    39	    return null;
    40	  }
    41	
    42	  const nonEmptyLines = (/** @type {string} */ s) => s.split(NEWLINE).filter((/** @type {string} */ l) => l !== '');
    43	  const dirtySummary = nonEmptyLines(status.stdout);
    44	  const untrackedPaths = nonEmptyLines(untrackedList.stdout);
    45	
    46	  const untrackedEntries = untrackedPaths.map((rel, i) => {
    47	    if (i >= UNTRACKED_CAP) return [rel, 'capped'].join(SEP);
    48	    try {
    49	      const content = fs.readFileSync(cwd + '/' + rel, 'utf8');
    50	      return [rel, String(Buffer.byteLength(content)), sha256(content)].join(SEP);
    51	    } catch {
    52	      // Listed but unreadable (deleted mid-flight): still digest its presence.
    53	      return [rel, 'absent'].join(SEP);
    54	    }
    55	  });
    56	
    57	  return {
    58	    branch: branch.stdout.trim(),
    59	    headSha: head.stdout.trim(),
    60	    dirty: dirtySummary.length > 0,
    61	    dirtySummary,
    62	    contentDigest: sha256([staged.stdout, unstaged.stdout, ...untrackedEntries].join(SEP)),
    63	  };
    64	}

    34	function captureTranscriptTail(root, raw, io) {
